- **sci-retr-web**: sci-retr skill 전용 — 웹 경로 목록(_collect/manual_download.csv) 전체를 사용자 Chrome(Claude in Chrome)에서 끝까지 받는다(본문 PDF·SI, PDF 없는 웹 전용 글과 PDF 받기에 여러 번 실패한 논문은 웹 본문). 확인 창이 뜨면 메인에게 알린다. 끝나면 intake·status 를 돌려 보고한다. sci-retr 를 따르는 메인이 웹 목록 전체를 하나에 맡긴다. ([angmond1/sci](https://github.com/angmond1/sci/blob/HEAD/sci-retr/agents/sci-retr-web.md))
- **sci-review-assumptions**: Fresh-eyes review of an experiment plan before it runs (a filled-in report template, a preregistration skeleton, or a design doc). Is the plan sound, and are its assumptions right? ([z0u/sca2](https://github.com/z0u/sca2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sci-review-assumptions.md) · ★1)
- **sci-review-claims**: Fresh-eyes review of an experiment report once its results are in (do the results support what the report says?). ([z0u/sca2](https://github.com/z0u/sca2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sci-review-claims.md) · ★1)
- **sci-review-style**: Fresh-eyes style review of an experiment report or preregistration skeleton (can a fresh reader follow it?). ([z0u/sca2](https://github.com/z0u/sca2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sci-review-style.md) · ★1)
- **sci-reviewer**: Adversarial audit of a scientific draft — FATAL/MAJOR/MINOR findings with inline annotations. Reads draft fresh (no writer context). Spawned by paper_pipeline.py after sci-verifier. ([krmdel/organon](https://github.com/krmdel/organon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sci-reviewer.md) · ★8)
- **sci-tldr-writer**: sci-tldr skill 전용 — 한줄요약 묶음 파일 하나(_collect/tldr_batch_&lt;n&gt;.md)를 읽고, 논문마다 한국어 한 문장 요약을 묶음에 적힌 JSONL 파일에 쓴다. 묶음 머리의 규칙(글에 있는 내용만, 숫자·물질명·화학식은 글 그대로)을 따른다. sci-tldr 을 따르는 메인이 묶음마다 하나씩 부른다. - **scio-refuter**: Adversarial checker for a Scio draft or panel material — assumes every claim is wrong and tries to prove it from the sources. Use with a lens (precision, weight or harm) before proposing and when reviewing. ([evisoft/scio.md](https://github.com/evisoft/scio.md/blob/HEAD/agents/scio-refuter.md) · ★5)
- **scio-refuter**: Adversarial checker for a Scio draft or panel material — assumes every claim is wrong and tries to prove it from the sources, with one lens (precision, weight or harm). Use before proposing a Scio article and inside a review seat. ([evisoft/scio.md.plugin](https://github.com/evisoft/scio.md.plugin/blob/HEAD/scio/agents/scio-refuter.md))
- **scio-researcher**: Finds reliable, independent sources for a Scio topic and judges whether it passes the notability test. Use as the first step of writing or maintaining an article. ([evisoft/scio.md](https://github.com/evisoft/scio.md/blob/HEAD/agents/scio-researcher.md) · ★5)
- **scio-researcher**: Finds reliable, independent sources for a Scio topic and judges whether it meets Scio's notability test (two independent in-depth reliable sources). Use as the first step of writing or maintaining a Scio article. ([evisoft/scio.md.plugin](https://github.com/evisoft/scio.md.plugin/blob/HEAD/scio/agents/scio-researcher.md))
- **scio-reviewer**: Blind reviewer for Scio panel assignments. Use when the main agent has pending panel assignments and wants them handled independently and carefully. ([evisoft/scio.md](https://github.com/evisoft/scio.md/blob/HEAD/agents/scio-reviewer.md) · ★5)
- **scio-reviewer**: Blind reviewer for the Scio panel seats assigned to the connected agent, one verdict per seat before its deadline. Use only when the user explicitly asks for their Scio seats to be answered without being shown each verdict first. ([evisoft/scio.md.plugin](https://github.com/evisoft/scio.md.plugin/blob/HEAD/scio/agents/scio-reviewer.md))
- **scio-writer**: Drafter for Scio — turns researched sources into a draft with one claim per sentence, then fixes what refuters flagged. Use in the writing team after scio-researcher; it drafts, it does not propose. ([evisoft/scio.md](https://github.com/evisoft/scio.md/blob/HEAD/agents/scio-writer.md) · ★5)
- **scio-writer**: Drafter for Scio — turns researched sources into an article draft in Scio's Markdown dialect with one claim per sentence and a matching claims list, then fixes what refuters flagged. Use in the Scio writing team after scio-researcher; it drafts, it never proposes. ([evisoft/scio.md.plugin](https://github.com/evisoft/scio.md.plugin/blob/HEAD/scio/agents/scio-writer.md))
- **scispark-orchestrator**: Scispark研究想法生成工作流编排智能体。通过7阶段结构化流程，将研究关键词转化为高质量、可验证的研究想法。包含文献检索、假设生成、技术优化、MoA分析和人机协作评审机制。 ([TashanGKD/cc_plugins](https://github.com/TashanGKD/cc_plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/scispark/agents/scispark-orchestrator.md) · ★4)
